       1100-BUILD-COUNTS.   

PPC470     IF TDP01-SCAT-SAIL-ID = TDP01-SAIL-ID AND
PPC470        TDP01-SAIL-CAT-CNT > 1
PPC470        CONTINUE  
PPC470     ELSE 
PPC470         PERFORM TDP01-RET-ALL-SCAT THRU  
PPC470                 TDP01-RET-ALL-SCAT-EXIT  
PPC470         IF TDP01-SCAT-SQLERR 
PPC470            GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
PPC470         END-IF   
PPC470     END-IF.  

           MOVE 'N'                      TO WS-CAT-FOUND.   

           PERFORM VARYING WS-CR-CAT-IDX FROM 1 BY 1
PPC470*        UNTIL WS-CR-CAT-IDX > SAIL1-CATEGORY-CNT 
PPC470         UNTIL WS-CR-CAT-IDX > TDP01-SAIL-CAT-CNT 
               OR WS-CAT-FOUND = 'Y'
               OR WS-CR-CAT-CODE (WS-CR-CAT-IDX) = SPACES   
               IF  WS-CR-CAT-CODE (WS-CR-CAT-IDX) = 
                   RSP15-O-CAT-CODE (WS-CR-RT-IDX  WS-CR-PPR-IDX)   
                   MOVE 'Y'              TO WS-CAT-FOUND
               END-IF   
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF  WS-CAT-FOUND = 'Y'   
               SUBTRACT 1                FROM WS-CR-CAT-IDX 
PPC470         COMPUTE WS-RSP15-CRUISE-RATE =   
PPC470          ((RSP15-O-CRUISE-AMT (WS-CR-RT-IDX  WS-CR-PPR-IDX 1) +  
PPC470            RSP15-O-CRUISE-AMT (WS-CR-RT-IDX  WS-CR-PPR-IDX 2) +  
PPC470           (RSP15-O-FLEX-PRC-ADJ-AMT  
PPC470                          (WS-CR-RT-IDX  WS-CR-PPR-IDX) * 2)) / 2)
PPC470*       IF  ( WS-CR-CRUISE-RATE (WS-CR-CAT-IDX) > 
PPC470*             RSP15-O-CRUISE-RATE (WS-CR-RT-IDX  WS-CR-PPR-IDX))  
PPC470*                                                             AND 
PPC470*           ( RSP15-O-CRUISE-RATE (WS-CR-RT-IDX  WS-CR-PPR-IDX)   
PPC470*             > ZEROS )   
PPC470        IF ( WS-CR-CRUISE-RATE (WS-CR-CAT-IDX) >  
PPC470             WS-RSP15-CRUISE-RATE AND 
PPC470             WS-RSP15-CRUISE-RATE > ZEROS)
PPC470*            MOVE RSP15-O-CRUISE-RATE 
PPC470*                  (WS-CR-RT-IDX  WS-CR-PPR-IDX)  
PPC470             MOVE WS-RSP15-CRUISE-RATE
                                         TO WS-CR-CRUISE-RATE   
                                            (WS-CR-CAT-IDX) 
                   MOVE RSP15-O-SUGG-POINTS 
                        (WS-CR-RT-IDX  WS-CR-PPR-IDX)   
                                         TO WS-CR-SUGG-POINTS   
                                            (WS-CR-CAT-IDX) 
               END-IF   
               GO TO 1110-ADD-COUNTS
           ELSE 
PPC470*        IF  WS-CR-CAT-IDX NOT > SAIL1-CATEGORY-CNT   
PPC470         IF  WS-CR-CAT-IDX NOT > TDP01-SAIL-CAT-CNT   
                   MOVE RSP15-O-CAT-CODE
                       (WS-CR-RT-IDX  WS-CR-PPR-IDX)
                                         TO WS-CR-CAT-CODE  
                                            (WS-CR-CAT-IDX) 
ppc470*           MOVE RSP15-O-CRUISE-RATE  
ppc470*               (WS-CR-RT-IDX  WS-CR-PPR-IDX) 
PPC470            COMPUTE WS-RSP15-CRUISE-RATE =
PPC470             ((RSP15-O-CRUISE-AMT (WS-CR-RT-IDX  WS-CR-PPR-IDX 1) 
ppc470                                                                 +
PPC470               RSP15-O-CRUISE-AMT (WS-CR-RT-IDX  WS-CR-PPR-IDX 2) 
PPC470                                                                 +
PPC470               (RSP15-O-FLEX-PRC-ADJ-AMT  
PPC470                         (WS-CR-RT-IDX  WS-CR-PPR-IDX) * 2)) / 2) 
ppc470            MOVE WS-RSP15-CRUISE-RATE 
                                         TO WS-CR-CRUISE-RATE   
                                            (WS-CR-CAT-IDX) 
                   MOVE RSP15-O-SUGG-POINTS 
                        (WS-CR-RT-IDX  WS-CR-PPR-IDX)   
                                         TO WS-CR-SUGG-POINTS   
                                            (WS-CR-CAT-IDX) 
                   GO TO 1110-ADD-COUNTS
               END-IF   
           END-IF.  

           GO TO 1100-BUILD-COUNTS-EXIT.

               UNTIL WS-CR-OCC-IDX > 5  
               ADD RSP15-O-REQ-CNT  
                   (WS-CR-RT-IDX  WS-CR-PPR-IDX WS-CR-OCC-IDX)  
                                         TO WS-CR-REQ-CNT

                   (WS-CR-RT-IDX  WS-CR-PPR-IDX WS-CR-OCC-IDX)  
                                         TO WS-CR-LOG-BKG-CNT   
                                            (WS-CR-CAT-IDX) 
                                            WS-BO-LOG-BKG-CNT   
                                           (WS-CR-CAT-IDX WS-CR-OCC-IDX)
PPC470*       ADD RSP15-O-BLK-SAILING-CNT   
PPC470*           (WS-CR-RT-IDX  WS-CR-PPR-IDX WS-CR-OCC-IDX)   
PPC470*                                 TO WS-CR-LOG-SAIL-CNT   
PPC470*                                    (WS-CR-CAT-IDX)  
PPC470*                                    WS-BO-LOG-SAIL-CNT   
PPC470*                                   (WS-CR-CAT-IDX WS-CR-OCC-IDX) 
PPC470        MOVE ZEROES               TO WS-CR-LOG-SAIL-CNT   
PPC470                                     (WS-CR-CAT-IDX)  
PPC470                                     WS-BO-LOG-SAIL-CNT   
PPC470                                    (WS-CR-CAT-IDX WS-CR-OCC-IDX) 
               ADD RSP15-O-BLK-GTEE-CNT 
                   (WS-CR-RT-IDX  WS-CR-PPR-IDX WS-CR-OCC-IDX)  
                                         TO WS-CR-BLK-GTEE-CNT  
                                            (WS-CR-CAT-IDX) 
                                            WS-BO-BLK-GTEE-CNT  
                                           (WS-CR-CAT-IDX WS-CR-OCC-IDX)
               ADD RSP15-O-BTH-GTEE-CNT 
                   (WS-CR-RT-IDX  WS-CR-PPR-IDX WS-CR-OCC-IDX)  
                                         TO WS-CR-BTH-GTEE-CNT  
                                            (WS-CR-CAT-IDX) 
                                            WS-BO-BTH-GTEE-CNT  
                                           (WS-CR-CAT-IDX WS-CR-OCC-IDX)
               ADD RSP15-O-BTH-CNT  
                   (WS-CR-RT-IDX  WS-CR-PPR-IDX WS-CR-OCC-IDX)  
                                         TO WS-CR-LOG-BTH-CNT   
                                            (WS-CR-CAT-IDX) 
                                            WS-BO-LOG-BTH-CNT   
                                           (WS-CR-CAT-IDX WS-CR-OCC-IDX)
               ADD RSP15-O-SOLD-CNT 
                   (WS-CR-RT-IDX  WS-CR-PPR-IDX WS-CR-OCC-IDX)  
                                         TO WS-BO-SOLD-CNT  
                                           (WS-CR-CAT-IDX WS-CR-OCC-IDX)
           END-PERFORM. 

       1100-BUILD-COUNTS-EXIT.  
           EXIT.

       1200-CALCULATE-COMPS.

           MOVE ZEROES                   TO WS-CP-NUM-GUESTS
                                            WS-RTE-CAT  
                                            WS-RTE-CAT-SUB. 
PPC470*    PERFORM VARYING WS-RTE-CAT FROM 1 BY 1   
PPC470*        UNTIL WS-RTE-CAT > RSP15-O-RTE-CATEGORY-CNT (WS-CP-RATE) 
PPC470*        IF  RSP15-O-RTE-CATEGORY-CODE (WS-CP-RATE WS-RTE-CAT) =  
PPC470*            RSP15-O-CPA-CAT-CODE (WS-CP-RATE WS-CP-CAT)  
PPC470*            MOVE  WS-RTE-CAT      TO WS-RTE-CAT-SUB  
PPC470*            MOVE  RSP15-O-RTE-CATEGORY-CNT (WS-CP-RATE)  
PPC470*                                  TO WS-RTE-CAT  
PPC470*        END-IF   
PPC470*    END-PERFORM. 

PPC470     PERFORM VARYING WS-RTE-CAT FROM 1 BY 1   
PPC470         UNTIL WS-RTE-CAT > RSP15-O-CATEGORY-CNT  
PPC470         IF  RSP15-O-CATEGORY-CODE (WS-RTE-CAT) = 
PPC470             RSP15-O-CPA-CAT-CODE (WS-CP-RATE WS-CP-CAT)  
PPC470             MOVE  WS-RTE-CAT      TO WS-RTE-CAT-SUB  
PPC470             MOVE  RSP15-O-CATEGORY-CNT   
PPC470                                   TO WS-RTE-CAT  
PPC470         END-IF   
PPC470     END-PERFORM. 

           PERFORM VARYING WS-CP-OCC FROM 1 BY 1
               UNTIL WS-CP-OCC > 5  
               IF  WS-CP-OCC = 1 AND
                   ( RSP15-O-SINGLES-PCT (WS-CP-RATE WS-CP-CAT) < 2.000)
                   COMPUTE WS-CP-NUM-GUESTS = WS-CP-NUM-GUESTS  
                                            + RSP15-O-REQ-CNT   
                                        (WS-CP-RATE WS-CP-CAT WS-CP-OCC)
               ELSE 
                   COMPUTE WS-CP-NUM-GUESTS = WS-CP-NUM-GUESTS  
                                            + ( RSP15-O-REQ-CNT 
                                        (WS-CP-RATE WS-CP-CAT WS-CP-OCC)
                                            * 2 )   
               END-IF   
           END-PERFORM. 

PPC470*    IF  WS-RTE-CAT-SUB > ZEROES AND  
PPC470*        ( RSP15-O-COMP-APPLY-IND (WS-CP-RATE WS-RTE-CAT-SUB) 
PPC470*          = 'Y' )
PPC470*        IF  ( RSP15-O-COMP-UNIT-TYPE (WS-CP-RATE WS-RTE-CAT-SUB) 
PPC470*              = 'C' )
PPC470*            COMPUTE WS-CP-DIVISOR = ( RSP15-O-NUM-COMPS  
PPC470*                                    (WS-CP-RATE WS-RTE-CAT-SUB)  
PPC470*                                  * 2)   
PPC470*                                  + ( RSP15-O-NUM-GUESTS-QUALIFY 
PPC470*                                    (WS-CP-RATE WS-RTE-CAT-SUB)  
PPC470*                                  * 2)   
PPC470*        ELSE 
PPC470*            COMPUTE WS-CP-DIVISOR = RSP15-O-NUM-COMPS
PPC470*                                    (WS-CP-RATE WS-RTE-CAT-SUB)  
PPC470*                                  + RSP15-O-NUM-GUESTS-QUALIFY   
PPC470*                                    (WS-CP-RATE WS-RTE-CAT-SUB)  
PPC470*        END-IF   
PPC470     IF  WS-RTE-CAT-SUB > ZEROES AND  
PPC470         ( RSP15-O-COMP-APPLY-IND (WS-RTE-CAT-SUB )   = 'Y' ) 
PPC470         IF  ( RSP15-O-COMP-UNIT-TYPE (WS-RTE-CAT-SUB) = 'C' )
PPC470             COMPUTE WS-CP-DIVISOR = ( RSP15-O-NUM-COMPS  
PPC470                                     (WS-RTE-CAT-SUB ) * 2)   
PPC470                                   + ( RSP15-O-NUM-GUESTS-QUALIFY 
PPC470                                     (WS-RTE-CAT-SUB) * 2)
PPC470         ELSE 
PPC470             COMPUTE WS-CP-DIVISOR = RSP15-O-NUM-COMPS
PPC470                                     (WS-RTE-CAT-SUB )
PPC470                                   + RSP15-O-NUM-GUESTS-QUALIFY   
PPC470                                     (WS-RTE-CAT-SUB )
PPC470         END-IF   
PPC470  
PPC470         COMPUTE WS-CP-GUESTS = WS-CP-GUESTS  
PPC470                              + WS-CP-NUM-GUESTS  
PPC470  
PPC470         IF  WS-CP-DIVISOR > 0
PPC470             COMPUTE WS-CP-RATIO = WS-CP-RATIO
PPC470                                 + ( WS-CP-NUM-GUESTS 
PPC470                                 /   WS-CP-DIVISOR )  
PPC470         ELSE 
PPC470             IF  CP-PROB-NOT-REPORTED 
PPC470                 MOVE 'Y'          TO WS-CP-PROB-REPORTED-FLAG
PPC470             END-IF   
PPC470         END-IF   
PPC470     END-IF.  

       1200-CALCULATE-COMPS-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

       1300-COMP-POLICY-RATIO.  

           MOVE 0                        TO WS-ALL-RATIO-ACCUMULATOR
                                            WS-ALL-DIVISOR. 
           MOVE 33141105                 TO DPP01-ERROR-NUMBER. 

           FETCH FIRST GRP1-BOOK-CAT-REC
               WITHIN GRP1-BOOKING-CAT-SET SET  
               ON ERROR GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR.  

           PERFORM VARYING WS-GBCR-CAT FROM 1 BY 1  
               UNTIL WS-GBCR-CAT > GBCR1-CATEGORY-CNT   
               ADD 1                     TO  WS-ALL-DIVISOR 
               COMPUTE WS-ALL-RATIO = GBCR1-NUM-COMPS (WS-GBCR-CAT) 
                                    / ( GBCR1-NUM-COMPS (WS-GBCR-CAT)   
                                    +   GBCR1-NUM-GUESTS-QUALIFY
                                        (WS-GBCR-CAT) ) 
               ADD WS-ALL-RATIO          TO WS-ALL-RATIO-ACCUMULATOR
           END-PERFORM. 

           COMPUTE YMBGBR-COMP-POLICY-RATIO-ALL =   
                                                WS-ALL-RATIO-ACCUMULATOR
                                                / WS-ALL-DIVISOR.   

       1300-COMP-POLICY-RATIO-EXIT. 
           EXIT.

       1400-CALC-COMPS. 

           INITIALIZE RSP03-COMP-INPUT  
                      RSP03-COMP-OUTPUT.

           MOVE ZERO                     TO WS-NUM-COMPS.   
           MOVE 'R'                      TO RSP03-I-BASED-ON.   

           CALL 'RSO03CMP' USING            DPP01-COMMON-DATA-FIELDS
                                            DPP01-COMMON-INFO   
                                            RSP15-CABIN-COUNTS-OUTPUT   
                                            RSP03-COMP-INPUT
                                            RSP03-COMP-OUTPUT.  

           IF  DPP01-SUB-MSG-CTR > 0
               DISPLAY DPP01-SUB-MSG-SOURCE (1) 
                  '  ' DPP01-SUB-MSG-INDEX (1)  
                  '  ' DPP01-SUB-MSG-STATUS(1)  
                  UPON PRINTER  
               DISPLAY 'ERROR IN SUBROUTINE RSO03CMP'   
                       '  PARAM-BOOK-NUM = ' PARAM-BOOK-NUM 
                  UPON PRINTER  
           END-IF.  

           IF  RSP03-O-TABLE-CNT > 0
               PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
                   UNTIL WS-SUB > RSP03-O-TABLE-CNT 
                   ADD RSP03-O-NUM-COMPS(WS-SUB)
                                         TO WS-NUM-COMPS
               END-PERFORM  
           END-IF.  

           MOVE WS-CP-GUESTS             TO YMBGBR-REQ-NUM-GUESTS.  
           MOVE WS-NUM-COMPS             TO YMBGBR-NUM-COMPS.   

       1400-CALC-COMPS-EXIT.

219528        GO TO 1800-RPOCESS-MAR-EXIT.  

219528*    IF  MAR-STATUS-CODE NOT = 'A'
219528*        GO TO 1800-RPOCESS-MAR   
219528     IF  IRP64-STATUS-CODE NOT = 'A'  
219528         GO TO 1800-NEXT-MAR  
           END-IF.  

219528*    IF  MAR-CODE NOT = 'BCMP' AND 'PCMP' 
219528*        GO TO 1800-RPOCESS-MAR   
219528     IF  IRP64-CODE NOT = 'BCMP' AND 'PCMP'   
219528         GO TO 1800-NEXT-MAR  
           END-IF.  

219528*    ADD MAR-ACTUAL-QUANTITY       TO WS-NUM-PAX  
219528     ADD IRP64-ACTUAL-QTY          TO WS-NUM-PAX  

219528*    GO TO 1800-RPOCESS-MAR.  
219528     GO TO 1800-NEXT-MAR.

PPC470*                                  TO WS-HOLD-SECOND-DATE 
PPC470*            WHEN 'D' 
PPC470*                MOVE DPP20-YYYYMMDD-DATE 
PPC470*                                  TO WS-HOLD-SECOND-DATE 
PPC470*            WHEN 'F' 
PPC470*                MOVE DPP20-YYYYMMDD-DATE 
PPC470*                                  TO WS-HOLD-FINAL-DATE  
PPC470*        END-EVALUATE 
PPC470*    END-PERFORM.

PPC470           UNTIL PMT-SUB > RSP15-O-PAYMENT-CNT
PPC470          OR RSP15-O-PAYMENT-CODE (PMT-SUB) NOT > SPACES

PPC470                  MOVE DPP20-YYYYMMDD-DATE
PPC470                                    TO WS-HOLD-FINAL-DATE 
PPC470          END-EVALUATE
PPC470      END-PERFORM.


           IF  ( WS-HOLD-FIRST-DATE-CC = 20  ) AND  
               ( WS-HOLD-FIRST-DATE-MM <= 12 ) AND

               MOVE WS-HOLD-FIRST-DATE   TO YMBGBR-FIRST-DEPOSIT-DATE   
           END-IF.

               ( WS-HOLD-FINAL-DATE-MM <= 12) AND

               MOVE WS-HOLD-FINAL-DATE   TO YMBGBR-FINAL-PAYMENT-DATE

           PERFORM VARYING RTE-SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
               UNTIL RTE-SUB > RSP15-O-RATE-CODE-CNT
PPC470*        PERFORM 2000-FETCH-MRR THRU  
PPC470*                2000-FETCH-MRR-EXIT

PPC470*           MOVE MRR-PTCHG-AMT    TO WS-PRT-CHG-AMT (RTE-SUB) 
PPC470*       END-IF

               PERFORM 2100-PROCESS-DEPOSIT-AMT THRU
                       2100-PROCESS-DEPOSIT-AMT-EXIT
                   VARYING CAT FROM 1 BY 1  
                   UNTIL CAT > RSP15-O-PRICE-POINT-CNT (RTE-SUB)
                   OR RSP15-O-CAT-CODE (RTE-SUB CAT) = SPACES

                                         TO RTE-SUB

       2100-PROCESS-DEPOSIT-AMT.

PPC470     MOVE 'N'                      TO WS-CAT-FOUND-SWITCH.
PPC470

PPC470         OR CAT-FOUND

       2110-PROCESS-PER-DEPOSIT.

PPC470*    MOVE 'N'                      TO WS-CAT-FOUND-SWITCH.
PPC470* 
PPC470*    PERFORM VARYING DEP-CAT FROM 1 BY 1  
PPC470*        UNTIL DEP-CAT > RSP15-O-CATEGORY-CNT 
PPC470*        OR CAT-FOUND 
PPC470*        IF  RSP15-O-CPA-CAT-CODE (RTE-SUB CAT) = 
PPC470*            RSP15-O-CATEGORY-CODE (DEP-CAT)  
PPC470*            MOVE 'Y'              TO WS-CAT-FOUND-SWITCH 
PPC470*        END-IF   
PPC470*    END-PERFORM. 
PPC470* 
PPC470*    IF  NOT CAT-FOUND
PPC470*        MOVE 'Y'                  TO WS-ERROR-SW 
PPC470*        MOVE RSP15-O-PRICE-POINT-CNT (RTE-SUB)   
PPC470*                                  TO CAT 
PPC470*        GO TO 2100-PROCESS-DEPOSIT-AMT-EXIT  
PPC470*    END-IF.  
PPC470* 
PPC470*    SUBTRACT 1 FROM DEP-CAT. 

PPC470*    PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB1 FROM 1 BY 1  
PPC470*        UNTIL WS-SUB1 > 2
PPC470*        IF  ( RSP15-O-COMMISSION-TYPE (DEP-CAT WS-SUB1) = 'I'
PPC470*                                                             AND 
PPC470*              RSP74-AGENCY-CONN-CODE = 'ER' OR 'IR'        ) OR  
PPC470*            ( RSP15-O-COMMISSION-TYPE (DEP-CAT WS-SUB1) = 'E'
PPC470*                                                             AND 
PPC470*              ( RSP74-AGENCY-CONN-CODE NOT = 'ER' AND 'IR' ) )   
PPC470*            COMPUTE WS-HOLD-PMT-AMT = RSP15-O-CRUISE-RATE
PPC470*                                     (RTE-SUB CAT)   
PPC470*                                    * RSP15-O-CRUISE-COMM-PCT
PPC470*                                     (DEP-CAT WS-SUB1)   
PPC470*        END-IF   
PPC470*    END-PERFORM. 

PPC470      COMPUTE WS-RSP15-CRUISE-RATE =  
PPC470          ((RSP15-O-CRUISE-AMT (RTE-SUB CAT 1) +  
PPC470            RSP15-O-CRUISE-AMT (RTE-SUB CAT 2) +  
PPC470            + (RSP15-O-FLEX-PRC-ADJ-AMT   
PPC470                         (RTE-SUB  CAT) * 2)) / 2).   

PPC470      COMPUTE WS-RSP15-PRT-CHG-AMT =  
PPC470          ((RSP15-O-NON-COMM-AMT (RTE-SUB CAT 1) +
PPC470            RSP15-O-NON-COMM-AMT (RTE-SUB CAT 2)   ) / 2).

PPC470     PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB1 FROM 1 BY 1  
PPC470         UNTIL WS-SUB1 > 2
PPC470         IF  ( RSP15-O-COMMISSION-TYPE (WS-SUB1) = 'I'
PPC470                                                              AND 
PPC470               RSP74-AGENCY-CONN-CODE = 'ER' OR 'IR'        ) OR  
PPC470             ( RSP15-O-COMMISSION-TYPE (WS-SUB1) = 'E'
PPC470                                                              AND 
PPC470               ( RSP74-AGENCY-CONN-CODE NOT = 'ER' AND 'IR' ) )   
PPC470*            COMPUTE WS-HOLD-PMT-AMT = RSP15-O-CRUISE-RATE
PPC470*                                     (RTE-SUB CAT)   
PPC470             COMPUTE WS-HOLD-PMT-AMT = WS-RSP15-CRUISE-RATE   
PPC470                                     * RSP15-O-CRUISE-COMM-PCT
PPC470                                      (WS-SUB1)   
PPC470         END-IF   
PPC470     END-PERFORM. 

PPC470*     COMPUTE WS-NET-PAYMENT-AMT = RSP15-O-CRUISE-RATE
PPC470*                                  (RTE-SUB CAT)  
PPC470      COMPUTE WS-NET-PAYMENT-AMT = WS-RSP15-CRUISE-RATE   
PPC470                                + WS-RSP15-PRT-CHG-AMT
PPC470*                               + WS-PRT-CHG-AMT (RTE-SUB)
                                      - WS-HOLD-PMT-AMT.

           PERFORM VARYING OCC FROM 1 BY 1  
               UNTIL OCC > 5
PPC470*        MOVE RSP15-O-PAYMENT-AMT (RTE-SUB CAT 1) 
PPC470*                                  TO WS-PAYMENT-AMT  
PPC470*        MOVE RSP15-O-PAYMENT-AMT (RTE-SUB CAT 2) 
PPC470*                                  TO WS-PAYMENT-AMT-1
PPC470         MOVE RSP15-O-PAYMENT-AMT (DEP-CAT 1 )
PPC470                                   TO WS-PAYMENT-AMT  
PPC470         MOVE RSP15-O-PAYMENT-AMT (DEP-CAT 2 )
PPC470                                   TO WS-PAYMENT-AMT-1


               COMPUTE WS-PAX-DEPOSIT = RSP15-O-REQ-CNT 
                                        (RTE-SUB CAT OCC)   
                                      * WS-PAYMENT-AMT  
                                      * WS-NET-PAYMENT-AMT * OCC

               COMPUTE WS-2-PAX-DEPOSIT = RSP15-O-REQ-CNT   
                                          (RTE-SUB CAT OCC) 
                                        * WS-PAYMENT-AMT-1  
                                        * WS-NET-PAYMENT-AMT * OCC  
               EVALUATE WS-DEPOSIT-IND  
                   WHEN ' D'
                       ADD WS-PAX-DEPOSIT   
                                         TO YMBGBR-SECOND-DEPOSIT-AMT   
                   WHEN '12'
                   WHEN '1D'
                       ADD WS-PAX-DEPOSIT   
                                         TO YMBGBR-FIRST-DEPOSIT-AMT
                       ADD WS-2-PAX-DEPOSIT 
                                         TO YMBGBR-SECOND-DEPOSIT-AMT   
               END-EVALUATE 
           END-PERFORM. 

       2120-PROCESS-AMT-DEPOSIT.

PPC470*    IF  RSP15-O-PAYMENT-AMT (RTE-SUB CAT 1) > 1  
PPC470     IF  RSP15-O-PAYMENT-AMT (DEP-CAT 1) > 1  
               PERFORM VARYING OCC FROM 1 BY 1  
                   UNTIL OCC > 5
PPC470*            MOVE RSP15-O-PAYMENT-AMT (RTE-SUB CAT 1) 
PPC470*                                  TO WS-PAYMENT-AMT  
PPC470*            MOVE RSP15-O-PAYMENT-AMT (RTE-SUB CAT 2) 
PPC470*                                  TO WS-PAYMENT-AMT-1

PPC470                                   TO WS-PAYMENT-AMT  
PPC470             MOVE RSP15-O-PAYMENT-AMT (DEP-CAT 2 )
PPC470                                   TO WS-PAYMENT-AMT-1
                   MOVE ZEROES           TO WS-CABIN-DEPOSIT
                                            WS-PAX-DEPOSIT  
                                            WS-2-CABIN-DEPOSIT  
                                            WS-2-PAX-DEPOSIT
PPC470*            IF  RSP15-O-PAY-TYPE (RTE-SUB 1) = 'C'   
PPC470             IF  RSP15-O-PAY-TYPE (1 ) = 'C'  
                       COMPUTE WS-CABIN-DEPOSIT = RSP15-O-REQ-CNT   
                                                  (RTE-SUB CAT OCC) 
                                                * WS-PAYMENT-AMT
                   ELSE 
                       COMPUTE WS-PAX-DEPOSIT = RSP15-O-REQ-CNT 
                                                (RTE-SUB CAT OCC)   
                                              * WS-PAYMENT-AMT  
                                              * OCC 
                   END-IF   
PPC470*             IF  RSP15-O-PAY-TYPE (RTE-SUB 2) = 'C'  
PPC470              IF  RSP15-O-PAY-TYPE ( 2 ) = 'C'
                       COMPUTE WS-2-CABIN-DEPOSIT = RSP15-O-REQ-CNT 
                                                    (RTE-SUB CAT OCC)   
                                                  * WS-PAYMENT-AMT-1
                   ELSE 
                       COMPUTE WS-2-PAX-DEPOSIT = RSP15-O-REQ-CNT   
                                                  (RTE-SUB CAT OCC) 
                                                * WS-PAYMENT-AMT-1  
                                                * OCC   
                   END-IF   
                   EVALUATE WS-DEPOSIT-IND  
                       WHEN ' D'
PPC470*                    IF  RSP15-O-PAY-TYPE (RTE-SUB 1) = 'C'   
PPC470                     IF  RSP15-O-PAY-TYPE (1) = 'C'   
                               ADD WS-CABIN-DEPOSIT 
                                         TO YMBGBR-SECOND-DEPOSIT-AMT   
                           ELSE 
                               ADD WS-PAX-DEPOSIT   
                                         TO YMBGBR-SECOND-DEPOSIT-AMT   
                           END-IF   
                       WHEN '12'
                       WHEN '1D'
PPC470*                      IF  RSP15-O-PAY-TYPE (RTE-SUB 1) = 'C  
PPC470                       IF  RSP15-O-PAY-TYPE (1 ) = 'C'
                                 ADD WS-CABIN-DEPOSIT   
                                         TO YMBGBR-FIRST-DEPOSIT-AMT
                             ELSE   
                                 ADD WS-PAX-DEPOSIT 
                                         TO YMBGBR-FIRST-DEPOSIT-AMT
                             END-IF 
PPC470*                       IF  RSP15-O-PAY-TYPE (RTE-SUB 2) = 'C 
PPC470                        IF  RSP15-O-PAY-TYPE (2 ) = 'C'   
                                 ADD WS-2-CABIN-DEPOSIT 
                                         TO YMBGBR-SECOND-DEPOSIT-AMT   
                             ELSE   
                                 ADD WS-2-PAX-DEPOSIT   
                                         TO YMBGBR-SECOND-DEPOSIT-AMT   
                             END-IF 
                   END-EVALUATE 
               END-PERFORM  
           END-IF.

202654               MOVE SPACES TO YMBGCRFX-REC (WS-CC-IDX:1)

CCLAUS*                                       RSP74-GB-BLENDED-XCHG-RATE

193385         END-IF

       8000-AMT-HOSED.  

            ADD 1                        TO ERROR-COUNTER.  
            MOVE SPACES                  TO ERROR-D-LINE.   
            MOVE 'NET FARE AMOUNT'       TO ERROR-D-LINE-FIELD-IN-ERROR.
            MOVE RSP74-NET-FARE-AMT      TO ERROR-D-LINE-ERR-VALUE. 
            MOVE RSP74-BOOK-NUM          TO ERROR-D-LINE-BOOK-NUM.  
            PERFORM 8100-WRITE-ERROR THRU   
                    8100-WRITE-ERROR-EXIT.  
            MOVE SPACES                  TO ERROR-D-LINE.   
            MOVE 'TOTAL AMOUNT'          TO ERROR-D-LINE-FIELD-IN-ERROR.
            MOVE RSP74-GB-GROSS-AMT-DUE  TO ERROR-D-LINE-ERR-VALUE. 
            PERFORM 8100-WRITE-ERROR THRU   
                    8100-WRITE-ERROR-EXIT.  
            MOVE SPACES                  TO ERROR-D-LINE.   
            PERFORM 8100-WRITE-ERROR THRU   
                    8100-WRITE-ERROR-EXIT.  

       8000-AMT-HOSED-EXIT. 
           EXIT.

       8100-WRITE-ERROR.

            IF  ERROR-PRINT-LINE-COUNTER > 50   
                MOVE 5                   TO ERROR-PRINT-LINE-COUNTER
                ADD 1                    TO ERROR-PRINT-PAGE-COUNTER
                MOVE ERROR-PRINT-PAGE-COUNTER   
                                         TO ERROR-HDG1-PAGE-OUT 
                WRITE YMBERR-REC FROM ERROR-HDG1
                WRITE YMBERR-REC FROM ERROR-HDG2
                WRITE YMBERR-REC FROM ERROR-HDG3
                WRITE YMBERR-REC FROM ERROR-UNDER-LINE  
                MOVE  SPACES             TO YMBERR-REC  
                WRITE YMBERR-REC
            END-IF. 

            ADD 1                        TO ERROR-PRINT-LINE-COUNTER
            WRITE YMBERR-REC FROM ERROR-D-LINE. 

       8100-WRITE-ERROR-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

       9000-END-OF-JOB. 

           IF  ERROR-COUNTER = 0
               MOVE ERROR-NO-ERRORS-LINE TO ERROR-D-LINE
               PERFORM 8100-WRITE-ERROR THRU
                       8100-WRITE-ERROR-EXIT
           END-IF.  

           MOVE 'CCLGBR'                 TO YMBGBR-CNT-RECORD-ID.   
           MOVE WS-DATESTAMP             TO YMBGBR-CNT-DATESTAMP.   
           MOVE ' COUNT: '               TO YMBGBR-CNT-FILLER-1.
           WRITE YMBGBR-CNT-REC.

           MOVE 'CCLGBF'                 TO YMBGBRFX-CNT-RECORD-ID. 
           MOVE WS-DATESTAMP             TO YMBGBRFX-CNT-DATESTAMP.

                 YMBGBRFX-FILE    YMBGBRFX-CNT-FILE
